# TrendOS — الكتاب الرئيسي القابل للتحديث

> **OWNER_TRANSITION_TWO_BOOKS_WRITE_GATE_20261016 — أمر المالك الملزم / Transitional Authority**
>
> **فرع التعايش الحالي:** `agent/trendos-book-audit-v1-20261014` — [الكتاب القديم النشط هنا](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/blob/agent/trendos-book-audit-v1-20261014/TrendOS_MASTER_BOOK.md) وبجانبه [الكتاب الموحد الجديد هنا](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/blob/agent/trendos-book-audit-v1-20261014/docs/trendos/book-agent/TRENDOS_CURRENT_SYSTEM_CATALOG.md). الكتابان على **نفس فرع العمل**.
>
> **القديم هو سجل الكتابة الوحيد حاليًا — ACTIVE_WRITE.** كل ما يُنفَّذ الآن (كود، أخطاء، اختبارات، PR، إصلاحات، قطع Google، دروس، تسليمات) يُوثَّق **هنا فقط** بتاريخ وأدلة SHA؛ لا تحول التسجيل المباشر إلى الكتاب الجديد أثناء الترحيل.
>
> **الجديد = FROZEN_READ_ONLY_FOR_NORMAL_AGENTS.** ممنوع أي إيجنت أو workflow أو دورية تكتب تلقائيًا للكتاب الجديد أو تعامل فصوله كحالة إنتاجية حديثة. **استثناء وحيد:** منفذ الترحيل المعيّن، عند نقل جزء مثبت من القديم على commit منفصل مع مطابقة كاملة بين أصل النص والأرشيف والوجهة. لا تغيير محتوى جديد في الموحد دون عملية ترحيل صريحة موثقة.
>
> **نقل كل جزء = COPY→VERIFY→REMOVE:** كل وحدة تنتقل إلى الجديد، ثم تُقرأ مقارنة كاملة (سطر/بصمة/حدود) ثم تُزال من القديم فقط بعد إثبات وجودها في الجديد، مع الاحتفاظ بفرع الاسترجاع `archive/trendos-books-before-single-book-20261016` وGit history. أي جزء لم يثبت نقله يظل في القديم ولا تُسقطه أي عملية تنظيف.
>
> **التحول النهائي CUTOVER:** فقط بعد `ALL_OLD_SECTIONS_MIGRATED=YES` و`NO_LOSS_CHECK=PASS` و`OWNER_APPROVAL=YES` يُحذف الكتاب القديم من فرع العمل ويُرفع حظر الكتابة عن الجديد ويصير هو المرجع الوحيد. حتى ذلك الحين: القديم للكتابة والجديد للقراءة والترحيل المراجع فقط.
>
> **حدود النطاق:** لا تمس كتب المشاريع المستقلة (بما فيها المطبعة الذاتية `autonomous-printshop/MASTER_BOOK.md`)، ولا تحذف ملفات تشغيل/اختبارات. ملف الفهرس/مسودات الكتب الوسيطة الخاصة بـTrendOS تُنقل إن كان فيها محتوى فريد ثم تُزال بعد التحقق؛ ملفات السجل التقني والاختبارات ليست كتبًا مستقلة.
>
> **مستوى القفل:** هذا عقد إلزامي للإيجنتات داخل Git، لكنه **ليس** GitHub branch-protection rule؛ منع GitHub التقني للـpush يحتاج إعداد صلاحيات/Ruleset منفصل. لا ندعي قفلًا على مستوى GitHub.
>


> **MASTER BOOK / Active Zero-Google Core**  
> إصدار الكتاب: **4.22-ZERO-GOOGLE-COMPACT — Entry642 deploy-resilient Auth hardening PASS** · تاريخ التحديث: 2026-10-07 · المستودع: `fawakhry/TrendOs` · فرع العمل: `candidate/t12-full-cloud-cutover-a56-20260929`.

> # ⚠️ اقرأ هذا أولًا — تعليمات إلزامية لأي شات أو مطور
>
> عند بدء أي شات جديد وطلب «اقرأ كتاب TrendOS»، **ابدأ بهذه التعليمات قبل أي تحليل أو تنفيذ**:
>
> 1. **ممنوع التخمين.** لا تستنتج حالة جزء من اسمه أو من وجود ملف كود. إذا لم يوجد دليل كافٍ، اكتب بوضوح: «غير مثبت بعد» وابحث عن الدليل.
> 2. **اقرأ الحي أولًا ثم الأرشيف عند الحاجة.** الحالة الحية في هذا الكتاب هي نقطة البداية، لكن أي بحث عن عطل/قرار/كود/وظيفة/تغيير قديم يجب أن يشمل فهرس الأرشيف في §8A وGit history والصندوق الأسود عند الحاجة.
> 3. **البحث موحّد: حي + أرشيف.** إذا طلب المالك «ابحث عن X»، ابحث أولًا في أقسام الكتاب الحية، ثم في المراجع المؤرشفة المرتبطة، ولا تقل «غير موجود» قبل فحص الاثنين. أعد النتيجة مع تحديد: حي / انتقالي / مؤرشف / غير مثبت.
> 4. **اشرح للمالك بالعربي البسيط.** النتيجة الأساسية تكون عربية مفهومة بدون إغراق في المصطلحات؛ ثم ضع أسماء الملفات والدوال والمسارات والـcommits التقنية الدقيقة عند الحاجة للمطور أو الصيانة.
> 5. **Runtime هو الحكم.** ترتيب قوة الدليل هو: `LATEST VERIFIED RUNTIME > DEPLOYED > TESTED > REPO-ONLY > HISTORICAL`. وجود كود أو خطة أو migration في GitHub لا يثبت أنه يعمل في Production.
> 6. **لا تُسقط أي جزء من المنصة.** الهدف النهائي للدليل هو تغطية كل شاشة، زر، action، function، ملف حي، Worker، جدول/حقل بيانات، dependency، مسار إدخال/إخراج، عطل معروف، طريقة تشخيص، وطريقة استرجاع.
> 7. **أي ميزة جديدة غير مكتملة بدون توثيقها.** لا تعتبر المهمة منتهية حتى تُضاف خريطتها للكتاب: ماذا تفعل → أين تظهر → نقطة الدخول → الكود → السيرفر → التخزين → النتيجة → الاعتمادات → التشخيص → الاسترجاع/rollback → الدليل الحي.
> 8. **لا تخلط الأرشيف بالحالة الحالية.** التاريخ مهم للبحث والتشخيص لكنه لا يصبح سلطة حالية إلا إذا أثبت Runtime أنه عاد للاستخدام.
> 9. **قبل أي تعديل Production:** تحقق read-only من الحالة الحالية، ثم سجّل النتيجة والخطوة بوضوح. لا تنفذ من ذاكرة شات قديم إذا اختلف الدليل الحالي.
> 10. **معيار اكتمال الكتاب:** لا يُقال إن الكتاب «كامل ومقفول» إلا بعد Coverage Audit يثبت: `LIVE_FUNCTION_WITHOUT_BOOK_MAP=0` و`LIVE_CODE_WITH_UNKNOWN_OWNER_OR_PURPOSE=0` و`LIVE_DATA_WITH_UNKNOWN_SOURCE_OR_DESTINATION=0`، مع تغطية البحث الحي والأرشيف.
>
> **قاعدة تسكين البحث بالعربي:** لكل جزء تقني يُضاف للكتاب، سجّل اسمًا عربيًا واضحًا ومرادفات البحث الشائعة بالعربي بجانب الاسم التقني، حتى يمكن الوصول إليه بالبحث العربي حتى لو كان اسم الملف أو الدالة بالإنجليزية.
>
> **فهرس المستودع الكامل:** `docs/trendos/TRENDOS_REPOSITORY_CATALOG.md` — جرد بحثي كامل للشجرة الحالية: **1,435 ملفًا + 35 مجلدًا = 1,470 مسارًا**، مع كلمات بحث بالعربي لكل مسار. استخدمه مع §8A للبحث في الحي والأرشيف، ولا تعتبر تصنيف المسار إثباتًا لحالة Production.

> **الغرض الحالي:** هذا الكتاب يصف **الحالة الحية، ما انتقل فعليًا من Google إلى Cloudflare/D1، وما بقي لإتمام Zero-Google**. التفاصيل التاريخية لمسارات Google التي أُغلقت أو استُبدلت لا تُكرر هنا؛ تبقى محفوظة في Git history والصندوق الأسود ووثائق الـEntry التفصيلية.

> **قاعدة الدليل:** `LATEST VERIFIED RUNTIME > DEPLOYED > TESTED > REPO-ONLY > HISTORICAL`. لا تعتبر الخطة أو الكود الموجود في GitHub إثباتًا لحالة Production.

> **قاعدة التسجيل:** كل خطوة جديدة تؤثر في Repo / Cloudflare / D1 / Apps Script / Production تُسجل هنا فورًا بالحالة الفعلية والدليل والخطوة التالية. ويشمل ذلك: كل محاولة ناجحة أو فاشلة، Runtime drift، rollback/restore، إصلاح hardening، workflow/run/job، deploy، تشخيص سبب الفشل، وفتح/إغلاق أي gate. لا تعتبر أي خطوة مكتملة قبل تسجيلها هنا. ممنوع تسجيل passwords أو Tokens أو password hashes أو session secrets.

## 1. الحالة النشطة — Production baseline بعد Entry600

### Entry596 — أوردرات Legacy قديمة ظاهرة Active رغم أنها مُسلّمة
- الاسم العربي للبحث: **الأوردرات القديمة لا تقفل / البند غير موجود / أوردرات مسلمة ظاهرة / stale legacy orders / legacy line identity**.
- Root cause مؤكد Read-only: D1 Lines mirror متوقف عند `2026-09-26 18:33:08` ويعرض **15 بندًا قديمًا كـ`طلب جديد`**، بينما نفس الـ15 بند في Google الحالي كلها **`تم التسليم`**.
- Google الحالي: 768 بندًا؛ Active = 0؛ `تم التسليم=639`، `جاهز للاستلام=73`، `مكرر=36`، `ملغى=20`.
- D1 read-only diagnosis: run `37018544009`, job `110875375520` = SUCCESS؛ D1 mirror source/row count = 768، stale-active = 15.
- الـ15 المتأثرة: `TM2606150097`, `TM2606150098`, `TM2606150105`, `TM2606160146`, و`4310, 4312-4321`.
- للأوردرات `4310..4321` ثبت date-coercion في Line ID؛ D1 يصلح الهوية للقراءة إلى مثل `4310-01` بينما raw legacy identity تاريخية، ومسار Apps Script `updateLine_` يفشل بالمقارنة ويعيد حرفيًا: `البند غير موجود في الشيت.`
- لا يوجد فقد للأوردرات نفسها؛ المشكلة **stale D1 snapshot + legacy Line-ID write mismatch**.
- ممنوع إصلاحها بالاعتماد على stale `rowNumber` لأن الصف قد يتحرك.
- المسار الدائم المعتمد للتأهيل: **D1-native legacy-line runtime overlay** يطبق status/notes فوق الـmirror قبل الفلترة، ثم ينقل legacy updateLine إلى Cloud؛ لا تعديل Apps Script v159.
- التشخيص لم يغيّر D1 أو Google أو Order Status أو Production.
- الحالة الحالية بعد Entry600: `ROOT_CAUSE_CONFIRMED=YES`, `PRODUCTION_FIX_DEPLOYED=YES`, `BUSINESS_RECONCILIATION_DONE=YES`, `STALE_15_RECONCILED=YES`.
- هذه المشكلة مغلقة Production؛ لا تُعاد معالجة الـ15 مرة أخرى.

### SERVER-20261016-61 — customer-manager D1 bridge malformed-response fallback (2026-10-16)
- **النوع:** TrendOS Customer Manager / Cloudflare legacy client bridge / SOURCE-ONLY؛ مزامنة معرفة من إيجنت Ubuntu بدون نسخ كتاب السيرفر كاملًا. لا تُنقل إلى الكتاب الموحد الجديد أثناء `OWNER_TRANSITION_TWO_BOOKS_WRITE_GATE_20261016`.
- **اكتشاف مؤكد باختبار وهمي فقط:** `customer_manager_d1_bridge_v1934.js` كان يعرض رد HTTP 200 ناقص المصفوفة المطلوبة (inbox `conversations` أو thread `messages`) على أنه قراءة D1 صحيحة بقائمة فارغة، بدل Apps Script fallback. هذا خلل في التعامل مع ردود غير مكتملة؛ ليس إثبات وقوع بيانات ناقصة في Production أو تسريب.
- **التعديل:** [Draft PR #184](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/pull/184)، الفرع `fix/server61-legacy-d1-malformed-read-fallback-20261016`، قائم فوق [Draft PR #176](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/pull/176). التعديل يتحقق من وجود المصفوفة قبل قبول البيانات، ويستخدم fallback الموجود عند غيابها؛ يحافظ على `[]` الصحيحة وعمليات `send/suggest/handoff/resolve` على Apps Script. Source commit `0379d6a`، اختبارات `64b2586`.
- **CI المؤكد:** [SERVER-56 compatibility Actions run 38949992665](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/38949992665) = `SUCCESS / 12 PASS / 0 FAIL`، منها 3 اختبارات SERVER-61 جديدة؛ [A61 transport run 38949992708](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/38949992708) = `SUCCESS`. هذه اختبارات Offline/Source-only وليست إثبات Runtime.
- **المرجع التفصيلي:** `autonomous-printshop/print-server/UBUNTU_SERVER_BOOK.md`، بند `SERVER-20261016-61` على فرع PR #184، توثيق CI commit `71501744835f99803a809136cda9101ab28bbf66`.
- **بوابات مفتوحة:** `SEC-AP303-02=OPEN_HIGH`؛ Worker `trendos` القديم ليس Worker `trendos-d1-api`، وإصدار `trendos` النشط ومصدره وحماية WAF/Access والمستهلكون الخارجيون لم يُتحقق منها. PRs #151/#154/#160/#176/#184 لم تُدمج وقت المراجعة. `CLOUDFLARE_DEPLOY=NO`، `D1_LIVE_PROBE=NO`، `UBUNTU_LIVE=NO`، `PRINT_LASER=OFF`.
- **الخطوة التالية:** تثبيت إصدار Worker `trendos` الفعلي بدليل Cloudflare read-only وربطه بمصدر الكود ومصفوفة المستهلكين، ثم مراجعة التصريح والتوافق والـrollback قبل أي Merge/Deploy بموافقة المالك.

## 2026-10-23 — Entry669 Zero Google finance safety (LIVE status: NO_GO)

- Reviewed current [PR #221](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/pull/221) (OPEN/DRAFT/unmerged), stacked on [PR #220](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/pull/220) (T12 preflight only). [PR #194](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/pull/194) BOOK24 writer qualification also unmerged. No production release was performed.
- **Implemented on existing PR221 branch**, not a second PR: prevent `Number(null)` / blank / string / boolean EasyStore balances silently becoming zero in `cloudflare-d1/src/customer-portal-easystore-finance-id-read-v0.mjs`; Node tests reject missing/non-numeric values and confirm true numeric zero, and Entry668 doc contains Entry669 note. Commits: [cfd61b5](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/cfd61b5eee73ce30c1b4bcc00c4952d179d64feb), [591cfe2](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/591cfe253d7888baac1958bc610e7639e6ad5b11), latest [6b85e81](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/6b85e81355eec4f52451d6839fa1fbcf0118f69c).
- **Exact-head CI success:** [Entry668 #39965608377](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39965608377) and [A61 #39965608403](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39965608403). Offline Node, disposable SQLite, earlier portal regression; **not** deployed Worker/EasyStore parity proof.
- **Finance blocked for production:** table `employee_accounting_party_balances_v1` is existing EasyStore/TrendOS boundary; customer Native `customer_id` to EasyStore `party_id` equality NOT independently attested in live records. Migration `0015` statement ledger has `party_name/party_code`, not dependable `party_id` for customer-scoped complete statements. No new financial writer/ledger allowed. Balance preview OFF; full invoice/transaction/debt parity and freshness need EasyStore owner approval + isolated stage readback. Missing balance never zero; boolean offline attestation is not production authorization.
- **T12 blocked for production:** existing `createT12GeneralOrder` only uses trusted employee Edge token, while preflight rejects multi-item and R2 file drafts to avoid dropping customer data. Needs safe trusted delegated actor, immutable snapshot, identical line/file mapping, existing idempotency ledger, single-writer proof, timeout ACK reconciliation; no new writer or employee token for customer.
- **Google/Cloudflare authority:** `legacy-browser-transport-v1.mjs` still forwards customer actions via `APPS_SCRIPT_API_URL` when legacy route invoked; Wrangler source still specifies that variable. No current-production Cloudflare deployment ID, D1 readback, R2 parity or signed finance mapping checked in this operation. `CUSTOMER_ACTIONS_RETIRED_LIVE_PROVEN=0/9`: seven prepared source functions, submit preflight only, finance preview only. `ZERO_GOOGLE_PRODUCTION=NO_GO`, `MERGED=NO`, `DEPLOYED=NO`, `GOOGLE_DELETE=NO`.
- **Pending decisions:** financial statement authoritative match (EasyStore), T12 secure delegation and complete draft-file transfer, BOOK24 Single Writer, production deploy controls/secrets, OTP/rate limiting, R2 >5MiB parity, separate stage and canary/rollback. ## 2026-10-23 — Entry680 exact D1 read transaction for native customer T12 handoff inventory

- **Source-only engineering change, no route/deploy/order:** Latest Entry679 handoff inventory previously read verified owner, 1..50 draft items, and 0..250 file metadata in three independent D1 round trips. A draft could mutate in between and yield an inconsistent digest. Converted it to three parameterized **SELECT-only** statements inside one `db.batch([owner,items,files])`. The official Cloudflare D1 API documentation specifies `batch()` as a single SQL transaction with sequential statements and rollback on failure (Docs: https://developers.cloudflare.com/d1/worker-api/d1-database/, last updated June 22 2026). No SQL mutations or browser route.
- A missing `db.batch`, failed statement (`success!==true`), missing/invalid results or thrown exception fail shut without falling back to old independent reads. Enforced in fake D1 Node unit tests using a stub that throws on `.first/.all`, plus disposable SQLite 3-SELECT SAVEPOINT contract tests using the original session/item/file tables. Existing 50 items, 250 files and strict READY/ownership/content metadata/ordinal/flags validation still required.
- Exact semantics: `transactionalReadBatch=true` means those D1 reads are **one consistent read transaction**. `snapshotSealed=false`, `atomicConsistencyVerified=false` (no live Worker verification), `r2FullBytesVerified=false`, `handoffReady=false`; immediately after batch the customer could still mutate a draft. A durable freeze/CAS or transactional ownership transfer is **still needed** before T12 canonical create. This is *not* exactly-once submission, multi-item T12 functionality, a new account/order system, or permanent file custody.
- Regression process: first PR220 candidate Head `20a7a7719720dffc500d78c9e645e53ba10ab1a8` failed its Entry667 CI (run #39987563093) solely because old unit test expected no file SELECT preparation on an invalid item, incompatible with all-queries-in-one-batch. Updated test to assert the batch occurs and the result fails closed; **exact-head retest** on PR [#220](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/pull/220) HEAD `8970adcf18c783a036ef5120fdc7116c4a72cc41`: [Entry667+679 run #39987677393](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39987677393), [Entry666/R2 run #39987676995](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39987676995), [A61 run #39987677108](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39987677108), all SUCCESS.
- PR [#221](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/pull/221) exact HEAD `c4b7e772e3e52d39ab0e59dde5b7f0968d468bfc`: [Entry667+679 run #39987684034](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39987684034), [Entry666/R2 run #39987684027](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39987684027), [Entry668/EasyStore finance run #39987683928](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39987683928), [A61 run #39987683903](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39987683903), all SUCCESS.

## 2026-10-23 — Entry681 native customer T12 preflight same-transaction ledger replay guard

- **Source code issue resolved, no production order created:** The source-only `preflightNativeCustomerDraftSubmitV0` previously read session/owner, draft items, R2 file count, and T12 ledger via independent Worker-D1 round trips. D1 mutation between those reads could make `preflightEligible` reference different source states and check the ledger later. Now all FOUR parameterized SELECTs run in a single Cloudflare D1 `db.batch` transaction; missing batch/partial or failed statement returns strict 503 without old-method fallback. Note that Cloudflare documents a D1 batch as a SQL transaction; actual live deployed code is not measured.
- T12 ledger status lookup is rewritten as a JOIN against `customer_portal_native_drafts_v1` constructing the **exact existing `cld1_<draft-created-ms>_<draft-hex>` key inside SQL** from the same snapshot. JS still validates request-key namespace, canonical T12 order shadow, strong registered phone, exact one item flags/notes, and explicitly rejects **any** R2 attachment and second draft item. Prior `PREPARED/COMMITTED/unknown` still needs separately authorized trusted readback and does NOT instruct retry or create a duplicate order.
- Source-only preflight returns `transactionalReadBatch:true`, `snapshotSealed:false` and `officialOrderCreated:false`. This eliminates **within-read** inconsistency only; immediately after the read any remaining customer draft mutations can occur, and T12 official writer delegation/atomic snapshot seal/full 50-line and R2 handoff remains absent. Single Writer BOOK24, EasyStore and Google release state unchanged.
- Updated fake D1 Node adapter to forbid separated `.first/.all` execution, test strict incomplete transaction response and owner/code/duplicate ledger/no-file/no-data-loss guards. Updated real disposable SQLite ledger query against actual D1 migrations; it derives the same key from the canonical draft timestamp and executes all four SELECTs inside SAVEPOINT.
- Intermediate CI failure disclosure: first new fake adapter code had a syntax error closing its nested mock `bind` method (PR #220 run #39988754740 and #221 #39988916831); repaired and ran exact-head workflows successfully. ## 2026-10-23 — Entry682 DB-enforced immutable customer draft seal on original T12 handoff branch

**Only source commits and exact-head GitHub Actions, NOT a deployed release.** Original old master `TrendOS_MASTER_BOOK.md` remains ACTIVE_WRITE; frozen catalog `docs/trendos/book-agent/TRENDOS_CURRENT_SYSTEM_CATALOG.md` was not touched. This is NEW SOURCE, not book migration.

### Why Entry682 exists
- Entry680/681 proved that source-only reads of owner/items/file count/ledger are consistent inside one D1 batch; they did **not** prevent the customer writing another line/file as soon as those reads finished. That TOCTOU hole prohibited trusting the observed digest for an official T12 create.

### What was actually developed
- Additive **OFF, un-applied** migration `cloudflare-d1/migrations/0039_customer_portal_native_draft_seal_v1.sql` defines `customer_portal_native_draft_seals_v1`, one immutable snapshot row per exact pre-existing `customer_portal_native_drafts_v1.draft_id`, with customer ID, canonical T12 cloud request key, private ordered `snapshot_json`, and timestamp. **10 SQLite triggers** deny later INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE on the original draft item and R2 file staging tables, draft UPDATE/DELETE, seal UPDATE/DELETE, whenever the matching seal exists. These triggers enforce database-level immutability independently of application worker route or readback code.
- `cloudflare-d1/src/customer-portal-native-draft-seal-v0.mjs` exports only a SOURCE-ONLY SQL contract, **not** a customer-facing handler or live service capability. A single guarded SQLite `INSERT OR IGNORE ... SELECT` authenticates the existing native customer D1 session, unique active customer portal code, D1 cloud-native source, GENERAL control, DRAFT state, 1..50 contiguous items, 0..250 READY files with exact owner/line/object key and <=5 files/item, and absence of any existing T12 ledger request. Snapshot is calculated within **same SQL INSERT** to avoid a separate read-versus-seal race. Repeated identical sealing does not make duplicate rows.
- Snapshot contains private customer identity, product notes and R2 object key metadata **inside D1 only**. It is neither a proof of R2 **body bytes** nor a customer-visible signed contract. No customer token can be passed as employee authorization; no `createT12GeneralOrder` call, no independent order sequence, finance ledger, Printshop job or Google service. No generic unseal/unlock/cancel; a controlled post-T12-COMMITTED transition and audited break-glass/compensation remain explicitly to be defined before owner approves staging/live migration.
- The new migration is not applied to D1 production, Cloudflare stage, R2 or other runtimes. Prior mutation SQL would be protected **only after** separately approved migration+seal; current deployed behavior is unchanged.

### Exact-head tests / provenance
- New `tests/entry682_customer_draft_immutable_seal_off.test.mjs` guards absent Worker route and double-writer; `tests/entry682_customer_draft_immutable_seal_sqlite.test.py` loads original D1 customer+draft+line+file schema on disposable SQLite and verifies: exact snapshot/unchanged official order count, duplicate seal no-op, late item/file mutation denied, draft/immutable seal edits denied, PENDING R2 blocks seal until READY, revoked/cross-customer session denied, existing T12 request and duplicate customer code denied, separate C2 draft unaffected, 50 ordered lines snapshot, post-seal revocation never unlocks data. Existing Entry667, Entry666, EasyStore and A61 regressions remained green.
- [PR #220](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/pull/220) HEAD `19f7164f4da1aff7e613f94977de7e726fefb352`: [Entry682 #39995214951](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39995214951), [Entry667 #39995215037](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39995215037), [Entry666/R2 #39995215128](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39995215128), [A61 #39995215055](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39995215055) all SUCCESS.
- [PR #221](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/pull/221) HEAD `98d4f34beb614776249f24b354cfc1552e1595d9`: [Entry682 #39995222974](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39995222974), [Entry667/T12 #39995222997](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39995222997), [Entry666/R2 #39995223108](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39995223108), [EasyStore finance #39995222884](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39995222884), [A61 #39995222921](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/39995222921) all SUCCESS exact head. ### Entry689 / ACC233 — V1 cryptographically signed ORIGINAL T12 order_created transport rehearsal (2026-10-23; SOURCE ONLY / NO_GO)

The **existing** V1 connector contract remains `fawakhry/EasyStore/docs/ACCOUNTING_CONNECTOR_CONTRACT_V1.json`: `order_created` is operational context only (`prepare_context_only`), not `order_line_approved` and NEVER a billable approval.

**Implementation:** New isolated `cloudflare-d1/src/t12-finance-connector-v1-order-created-sourceonly.mjs` imports the **original actual** source inspector `inspectT12EmployeeOrderFinanceCandidate()` directly, performs the same one-batch five-SELECT ACC228–232 safety qualification (COMMITTED at-create customer candidate/lines, original activity/outbox, CURRENT runtime cancellation veto), and returns a narrowly whitelisted connector V1 envelope `order_created` with stable IDs, single official native source **candidate** customer ID, exact original Print/Wael and Laser/Gaber line IDs/departments/ordinals, source-only/nonfinancial flags and **NO personal names/phones/prices**. An opt-in `ISOLATED_SOURCE_ONLY` function can HMAC-SHA256 sign its deterministic key-sorted canonical JSON using a caller-supplied **WebCrypto secret CryptoKey** and key ID. No key/secret created or embedded in source; no env binding, new route, Worker import, live backend, Cloudflare access or HTTP sender. Companion offline verifier requires **pinned caller-specified** key ID and HMAC verify key, exact V1 fields (no extra fields), correct source/customer/order/lines, no forbidden approval/financial fields and bounded issue-time freshness. Signature authenticity here means **only bytes verified with a supplied fake key**; neither the signer nor a successful verify attests a deployed trusted producer, official CUSTOMER↔order ownership, department approval, financial permission or live D1 provenance.

**Important delivery/authority limits:** `event_id` and `idempotency_key` are deterministic per T12 order; timestamp currently comes from the isolated signing invocation. A true at-least-once connector MUST durably persist/reuse the **same original signed envelope** before retry so timestamp changes do not turn a single idempotency key into conflicting payloads; such an outbox, receiver duplicate ledger, independent key custody/distribution, anti-replay, rotation, source/finance cloud attestation and signed real `order_line_approved` are **NOT implemented or qualified** here. Never describe `order_created` as a finalized invoice, verified ownership or signed department approval; the original EasyStore offline preview explicitly expects `order_line_approved` and this event must NOT be relabelled to bypass it. The existing T12 operational `queue:01/02` events are also **NOT** approvals. This is cryptographic **contract rehearsal** before genuine transport, not a live connector.

**Evidence:** Module created in commit [`84525de6`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/84525de64f437ff5174ae36277b4b1f5adb1ff36), existing original T12 SQLite test extended in [`63b99c27`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/63b99c279e3d17fa9be0db9929e2825677b84373). Nine new synthetic tests verify default OFF/non-wiring; real WebCrypto HMAC on isolated original SQLite source; exact V1 source IDs and no personal/financial fields; deterministic same-envelope retries; denied wrong signer mode/key/customer/source; cancellation veto; tampering of customer/line/event/finance flag; wrong pinned key/bad signature; future/stale expiry; and original line drift. ### Entry690 / ACC234 — stable original T12 source-time signed V1 order_created retries (2026-10-23; SOURCE ONLY / NO_GO)

**Owner scope and non-overlap:** EasyStore accounting agent independently provisions EasyStore finance DB/Stage. This agent changes ONLY the existing TrendOS PR #211 original T12 read-only source reader, existing source-only HMAC `order_created` rehearsal and original synthetic tests. No EasyStore source/financial database/Cloudflare/Ubuntu mutation; no merge/deploy, money/credit/invoice POST, signer deployment, key material, Google retirement, new ledger, migration, token or live customer data.

**Specific ACC233 defect closed:** The existing V1 `order_created` message used `occurred_at` derived from **each current signing attempt**, despite `event_id` and `idempotency_key` being deterministic per T12 order. Retries at different seconds could produce a **different HMAC canonical payload for the same idempotency key**, a future receiver replay conflict even though source order had not changed. This is not a transport or actual delivery proof.

**Actual source truth:** `0005_t12_production_create_canary.sql` already stores `created_at` on original COMMITTED request ledger, source order, original lines, original `t12_prod_events` activity, and original per-line `t12_prod_outbox`. The existing original `createT12GeneralOrder()` inserts these in one D1 `batch`. No extra SQL call: the five existing parameterized SELECTs in `cloudflare-d1/src/t12-employee-order-finance-intake-sourceonly.mjs` now retrieve those persisted timestamps in their original rows, validate strictly SQLite UTC `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS`, reject malformed/ambiguous/nonexistent dates and >60-second cross-record differences plus reversed ledger→order→activity ordering. The original activity `created_at` is returned as `sourceCreatedAt` **only** after original source/line/outbox/current-runtime checks pass; it has no customer ownership or invoice authority. These timestamps are persisted D1 observations and cross-checked, **not** tamperproof externally attested financial records (there is no separately trusted D1 timestamp signer). The source reader remains `SOURCE_ONLY`, `readOnly`, no new endpoint.

**Stable HMAC behavior:** The pre-existing `cloudflare-d1/src/t12-finance-connector-v1-order-created-sourceonly.mjs` now constructs `envelope.occurred_at` exclusively from the validated source `sourceCreatedAt`, not `Date.now()`/`nowMs`. Different signing invocation times with the same T12 source, same fake supplied HMAC key/key ID now produce exactly identical connector canonical payload and signatures. Both signer and existing verifier fail closed when the original source occurred_at is more than 300 seconds in the past or >60 seconds into the future. No current-date adjustment/re-sign of historical orders permitted, and no grace token invented. **This deliberately does not solve durable outbox or retransmission after the 5-minute expiry;** implementing a true at-least-once connector requires one immutable persisted pre-signed message, source key custody + rotation, pinned trusted producer binding, duplicate receiver ledger and real replay/ack/dead-letter before first dispatch. The original T12 `queue:01/02` **is not** that signed financial connector outbox.

**Execution/evidence:** Source timestamp verifier commit [`f0d49f40`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/f0d49f40aaacbcea3af279d2b9818701c2675b18); HMAC signer source-time commit [`c17fa3ea`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/c17fa3eae8ad80804bf15742061b8be8de5e463e); original test fixture/new replay-negative tests commit [`d69134ab`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/d69134ab9db0c0a777029388bd992fd6bb5034c8). The **first CI run correctly detected a double-escaped digit regex** in the newly added SQLite UTC parser: 24/34 expected source checks failed closed (not production effects). Fixed only the parser in [`0f1b4eb0`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/0f1b4eb01ca9ad6b1f745e1229c35b27dc553d6a) and **re-ran** original [ACC213 Action #40019428440](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/40019428440): source/replay tests **34/34 PASS**, zero fails, all **17/17 PR GitHub Actions completed SUCCESS at exact final source HEAD** `0f1b4eb01ca9ad6b1f745e1229c35b27dc553d6a`. Four new ACC234 test cases additionally check variable retry time reproducing identical bytes, signing/verification expiry, changed ledger/order/line/outbox/activity time fail-closed and malformed/future time; 30 preexisting cases preserved.

**Still unqualified:** Actual source-issued signed `order_line_approved` with authentic verified Wael/Gaber department grants/prices, independent official order↔customer ownership attestation, EasyStore-only invoice/cash/grants and its independently provisioned Stage, dedicated durable signed connector event outbox, authenticated LIVE Cloud-to-Cloud receiver and replay/dead-letter. No approval was inferred from T12 runtime state or operational outbox. `FINANCE_RELEASE=NO_GO`, `T12_ORDER_CREATED_SOURCE_TIME=SOURCE_ONLY_VERIFIED`, `DURABLE_AT_LEAST_ONCE_CONNECTOR=NOT_IMPLEMENTED`, `DEPARTMENT_APPROVALS=NOT_ATTESTED`, `G1..G5=OPEN`.


### Entry691 / ACC235-OUTBOX — immutable signed `order_created` fixture journal and safe retry simulation (2026-10-23; SOURCE ONLY / NO_GO)

**Division of responsibility and concurrent agents:** The EasyStore accounting agent owns the independent finance D1/Stage; a separate TrendOS agent concurrently implemented **another ACC235**, the native official customer identity boundary. This entry explicitly identifies **ACC235-OUTBOX** as a distinct transport-safety rehearsal and **does not overwrite or supersede** that customer's ACC235 commit, books or tests. All previous Entry683–690 records remain. No EasyStore/Cloudflare/Ubuntu production DB, invoice, ledger, balances, migrations, key distribution or deployment touched.

**Gap:** Existing ACC233/234 original T12 inspector-backed HMAC V1 `order_created` proof gives deterministic signed bytes and source timestamp within its 5-minute expiry. It does NOT persist the signed bytes for a crash, control two competing senders, or isolate an unacknowledged dead letter. Retry by rebuilding after TTL fails closed; the original `t12_prod_outbox` contains only `trend-master-status-intent` operational queue messages, **not** finance transport or real department approval.

**Change (not production schema):** Added `cloudflare-d1/src/t12-finance-signed-outbox-acc235-offline.mjs` on existing Draft PR211. Its exported `ACC235_ISOLATED_SCHEMA` is **created only in temporary disposable SQLite test databases, not in `cloudflare-d1/migrations` or any Worker D1**. Exported mode is OFF; explicit `ISOLATED_SQLITE_FIXTURE` only. Enqueue function invokes the REAL original T12 source-inspector-backed ACC233/234 HMAC signer with a fake caller-supplied WebCrypto key, independently verifies its result against pinned fake verification key, then `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` into an isolated signed-message fixture with UNIQUE event ID and idempotency key, stored exact JSON and SHA-256. After insert or duplicate, it re-reads and demands **exact stored byte/hash/order/idempotency parity**, rejects any payload conflict, and has SQL triggers disallowing signed-byte update/delete. No direct finance DB shared or monetary fields/price/customer-ownership/approval escalation.

**Retry/crash semantics:** A fixture-only conditional SQL `UPDATE ... RETURNING` leases the exact **persisted original signed bytes** to one simulated worker, checks SHA-256+HMAC pinned-key authenticity and creation-time freshness before leasing, increments bounded attempts, refuses live competing leases, allows a timed-out lease to be reclaimed, and supports explicit lease release when no delivery occurred. After bounded attempts a stalled message can be moved to `DEAD_LETTER` with original signed bytes preserved. **There is deliberately NO consumer ACK tool**: a fake API response or worker log cannot assert EasyStore receipt, ledger, true financial owner or posting. No external `fetch`, no Cloudflare Worker wiring, no scheduled retry service, no real sender, no receipting. Stage-from-source read and disconnected outbox insert are **NOT atomic**; a live implementation requires a source transaction/version/commit boundary plus actual durable deployed DB and signed receiver ACK/replay ledger before dispatch. Expired original event cannot be sent/re-signed later using this rehearsal.

**Evidence:** Isolated module [`333abdf9`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/333abdf9d61c87c9fe56cfa01a7d8a34072065b0); six original fake-SQLite source tests appended [`19390891`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/193908917f349d5446c62eda1db2b0008c5bbc6c). New isolated [ACC235-OUTBOX CI workflow `ef4ff8a4`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/ef4ff8a469527302591673a6fc1a7e86a9f4fccf) explicitly runs original source + HMAC + fixture checks without Cloudflare tokens/production and keeps `FINANCE_RELEASE=NO_GO`. [ACC235 offline GitHub Actions run #40020786740](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/40020786740) **SUCCESS, source tests 40/40 PASS** including 6 new outbox tests: OFF/no Worker wiring, exact immutable bytes and original DB zero writes, identical event across repeated signer time, CAS one-owner timeout-recovery bytes, bounded attempts/dead-letter without ACK, pinned key and stale event denial, actual close/reopen of disposable on-disk SQLite and competing requests with exactly one lease. The earlier concurrent accounting `import` regression caused multiple *other* inherited PR checks to fail on prior HEAD; the concurrently working accounting agent separately corrected its native VM adapter in [`e5016dc7`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/e5016dc74ff1b87d570941e47343022b5b8bede5). Do not claim earlier red accounting checks were caused/fixed by this outbox code.

**Open gates:** independently attested official T12 customer↔order ownership, real source-owned signed `order_line_approved` by verifiably authorized Wael/Gaber, official department pricing/role control, native EasyStore-only invoice policy and separate provider, verified customer financial balances, atomic *live* signed outbox and authenticated server-to-server transport, receiver idempotency plus persistent ACK/dead-letter. `ACC235_OUTBOX=DISPOSABLE_SQLITE_TESTED_ONLY`; `LIVE_CONNECTOR_TRANSPORT=OFF`; `FINANCE_RECEIPT_VERIFIED=NO`; `DEPARTMENT_APPROVALS_VERIFIED=NO`; `FINANCE_RELEASE=NO_GO`; all G1..G5 remain open.

## ENTRY694 — الفصل الدائم للمشاكل والاختبارات المؤجلة | سجل العيوب والإصلاحات والتجارب | TRENDOS_ISSUE_TEST_LEDGER_V1 (أمر المالك 2026-10-24)

> **OWNER RULE — إلزامي لكل شات/إيجنت جديد يعمل على TrendOS:** قبل كتابة كود أو اقتراح إصلاح أو إعادة اختبار، اقرأ هذا الفصل وآخر إضافاته التي تبدأ `ENTRY694-LEDGER-EVENT`، ثم سجل هنا **فورًا** كل مشكلة أو احتمال خلل أو اختبار فاشل أو مؤجل أو إصلاح تم، حتى لو ظهر أثناء الكتابة فقط. **لا تُغلق مشكلة لمجرد كتابة الإصلاح أو نجاح المحاكاة.** سجل أين توقفنا وما هو الدليل التالي اللازم. يسري الأمر على جميع محادثات المستقبل داخل حدود سلطة المشروع، وليس على كتب المشاريع المستقلة.
>
> **مكان الكتابة الوحيد الآن:** هذا `TrendOS_MASTER_BOOK.md` على `agent/trendos-book-audit-v1-20261014` = `ACTIVE_WRITE`. الكتاب الموحّد `docs/trendos/book-agent/TRENDOS_CURRENT_SYSTEM_CATALOG.md` = `FROZEN_MIGRATION_ONLY`، ممنوع التعديل المباشر فيه. سجل AGENT_LOG + مفاتيح Crosswalk/Manifest الجديدة بالتوازي؛ الأرشيف محفوظ ولا تُحذف الفصول القديمة.
>
> **حالة الدليل لا تُترقى بالتخمين:** `REPO/SOURCE < MOCK < NATIVE_NODE < CI < STAGING < DEPLOYED < VERIFIED_PRODUCTION`، مع التنبيه أن هذه مراحل إثبات مختلفة وليست بدائل متساوية. حالة `FIX_IN_DRAFT` ليست `FIX_DEPLOYED`؛ حالة `19/19` أو `10/10` هنا **اختبارات محاكاة فقط**، لا تعني نجاح CI أو Stage أو حماية Production.

### ENTRY694-A — بروتوكول التسجيل والمتابعة الملزم

1. **قبل البدء:** اقرأ HEAD الحالي، هذا الفصل والإضافات الأحدث، كتاب كل جزء في نطاقه، PRs النشطة، والاختبارات الموثقة؛ لا تعيد اختبار حالة سبق إثباتها بلا تغيير في المصدر أو هدف واضح. احتفظ بسجل ما فشل أيضًا.
2. **عند أي اكتشاف:** امنحه ID دائمًا (مثل `SEC-692-01` أو `CFL-693-03`). سجل: التاريخ، العنوان والاسم العربي/المرادفات، المسار/المكوّن، درجة الخطورة (مبدئية أو مثبتة)، إعادة الإنتاج ببيانات وهمية فقط، الأثر المتوقع مقابل ما ثبُت حيًا، السبب أو الفرضية، صاحب القرار، الرابط وcommit/blob/run الدقيق. لا تسرد أسرارًا أو معلومات عميل.
3. **عند الإصلاح:** سجل `NOT_STARTED → FIX_IN_DRAFT → CI → STAGE → DEPLOYED → VERIFIED` مع روابط كل دليل؛ لا تغيّر `PRODUCTION` إلى PASS إلا بدليل النسخة المنشورة والاختبارات المصرح بها. إذا ظهر فشل جديد، سجل حادثة/إضافة جديدة بدل طمس النتيجة القديمة.
4. **الاختبارات المؤجلة:** كل حالة لها ID ومعيار قبول وبيئة/مسؤول وحالة `NOT_RUN`، وتظل مفتوحة لحد نتيجة حقيقية موثقة. اختبارات رفض الوصول **قبل** R2/D1 والتكامل بالواجهة لا تُستبدل بفحص Health.
5. **كيف تسجل لاحقًا بدون فقد:** أضف في **نهاية الكتاب** عنوان `### ENTRY694-LEDGER-EVENT-YYYYMMDD-N — [issue ID / update]` بحقول `Issue | discovery/fix | evidence | tests run/results | tests not run | blocked | owner | next | status | SHA`. لا تعدّل أو تمسح هذا الفصل/نتائج التاريخ. أضف AGENT_LOG وCrosswalk وManifest بمفاتيح جديدة، واكتب فقط على HEAD الذي قرأته باستخدام `expected_sha`.
6. **قاعدة الإغلاق:** `CLOSED_WITH_PROOF` فقط عند وجود اختبار مناسب لنطاق المشكلة، وإثبات النسخة/الصلاحيات والـrollback عند تعلقها بالإنتاج، وموافقة المالك على أي نشر. إذا لا يوجد نشر فلا تضع `CLOSED_IN_PRODUCTION`. ملكية حسابات EasyStore لوكيل الحسابات، ولا ينقل هذا الفصل سلطة مالية.
7. **التحقق من التسجيل نفسه:** مقارنة GitHub تظهر الملفات الأربعة فقط وإضافات بلا حذف؛ Blob الكتاب المجمّد وفرع الأرشيف لم يتغيرا. أي تعارض كتابات يُعاد من HEAD الجديد؛ لا قوة إجبارية أو overwrites.

**مراجع الإصلاحات المثبتة:** [PR #234](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/pull/234) Draft HEAD `417298488e3f5f0d999cdce0b0d5279a967cfb85` = إصلاح صلاحيات Comms/Content/Mirror/T12 + ملف اختبارات `tests/entry692_private_read_guards.test.mjs`؛ [PR #236](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/pull/236) Draft HEAD `596ec59b0ace0beecdfa4d601dd80860a9a239d1` = حماية Rollback وWorkflow Bindings + ملف اختبارات `tests/entry693_cloudflare_guard_source.test.mjs`. كلاهما موجه إلى فرع PR #221، **Draft، غير مدموج وغير منشور**. اختبار Comms/Content/Mirror/T12 المعزول `19/19`، واختبار rollback المعزول `10/10`، مجموع **29 سيناريو محاكاة** لا تُخلط مع CI. خلال اختبار الصلاحيات الأول ظهر فشل cross-uploader لملاحظات Content فتم تضييق الشرط داخل PR #234 ثم أعيدت المحاكاة ونجحت؛ **الفشل الأول محفوظ في سجل Entry692 ولا يُحذف**.

**حدود مهمة لا تُسقطها عبارة «الإصلاح اتعمل»:** بعض روابط الملفات القديمة قد تحتوي مسار R2 مباشر ولا يغيرها كود رفع الملف الجديد؛ ملفات Content القديمة لا تحمل SHA256 مثبتة؛ `<img src>` لا يرسل Bearer تلقائيًا؛ عملاء Mirror الحاليون و02CW parity سيحتاجون تكييفًا آمنًا قبل حجب GET؛ مفتاح `MIRROR_READ_SECRET` لا يضاف للواجهة؛ PR #236 لا يمنع الكتابة خارج GitHub أو يحول GET→POST إلى CAS ذري؛ ملف Wrangler الحالي بلا FILES رغم أن القيد الجديد يطلبه، فيجب إبقاء النشر محظورًا حتى إثبات إعدادات Cloudflare فعلًا. لا يُستنتج من غياب FILES في المستودع غيابه من Worker المنشور.

### ENTRY694-C — قائمة اختبارات «لازم تتعمل بعدين» ومعيار النجاح (مفتوحة)

| Test ID | أين وماذا؟ | معيار النجاح الذي يسجل عند التنفيذ | وضعها الآن |
|---|---|---|---|
| `NODE_692` | tests/entry692_private_read_guards.test.mjs | Execute native Node against PR234 tree and receive passing assertions; preserve full logs + commit SHA | `NOT_RUN` |
| `NODE_693` | tests/entry693_cloudflare_guard_source.test.mjs | Execute native Node against PR236 tree, 10/10 negative/positive cases with trace | `NOT_RUN` |
| `EXISTING_REGRESSION_SUITE` | Entry614 Comms/Content, Entry629/630 dispatch, T12 overlay, Mirror gate | All relevant old tests pass after code changes; no accidental change of unrelated paths | `NOT_RUN` |
| `CI_YAML` | PR234 + PR236 check suites and workflows | GitHub CI jobs pass from exact PR SHAs and YAML correctly parsed; no workflows touching production invoked | `NOT_RUN` |
| `STAGE_R2_ACL_SHA` | synthetic Stage R2 bucket and D1 | authorized matching bytes/hash 200; revoked/other employee denied before FILES.get; missing/mismatch digest rejects; zero public exposure | `NOT_RUN` |
| `STAGE_CONTENT_PUBLIC_PRIVATE` | Stage Content active/inactive/notes | public ad works anonymously; inactive/private deny; uploader/admin authorized; cache headers correct | `NOT_RUN` |
| `FRONTEND_AUTH_DOWNLOAD` | protected proof download and preview UI | Bearer + employee identity sent via fetch, not query string; no token leakage to browser URL; explicit safe object URL lifecycle | `NOT_RUN` |
| `FRONTEND_IMAGE_COMPAT` | public marketplace/ad images and internal content | normal public marketing images render; private assets cannot render anonymously; old content UX reviewed | `NOT_RUN` |
| `MIRROR_READER_CLIENT_MIGRATION` | read-only mirror consumers incl. health/parity | inventory and migrate server-to-server consumers; never embed MIRROR_READ_SECRET in frontend; default-deny for missing config | `NOT_RUN` |
| `MIRROR_E2E` | Stage Mirror GET/POST | unauthorized GET ->401 without DB read, authorized read-only GET works, migration secret alone not valid for read, POST remains separately protected | `NOT_RUN` |
| `EDGE_T12_STAGE_ROLE_MATRIX` | T12 orders / Edge roles | empty and mismatched screen forbidden; matching role/screen allowed; expired/revoked sessions characterized with native validator | `NOT_RUN` |
| `LEGACY_PUBLIC_URL_READONLY_INVENTORY` | legacy file metadata only | owner-approved read-only number of raw R2 URL records, no customer objects/binaries copied or publicized | `NOT_RUN` |
| `CONTENT_DIGEST_SCHEMA_PROVENANCE` | Content file legacy SHA metadata | tracked schema and hashing migration strategy with positive/negative fixture, no implied digest for old Content files | `NOT_RUN` |
| `WRANGLER_R2_SOURCE_PARITY` | cloudflare-d1/wrangler.toml and effective live Worker config | owner-reviewed target Worker, DB, exact FILES bucket before enabling deploy; no guessed bucket string treated as live proof | `NOT_RUN` |
| `CLOUDFLARE_LIVE_BINDINGS_READONLY` | Cloudflare read-only API after owner permission | capture active deployment version/script etag and effective FILES+DB binding without revealing secrets; confirm correct account/target | `NOT_RUN` |
| `STAGE_CODEONLY_R2_PERSISTENCE` | disposable isolated Worker | FILES remains bound after approved code-only Wrangler deploy, positive authorized object roundtrip, negative private-object denial | `NOT_RUN` |
| `STAGE_CLOUDFLARE_FAILURE_MATRIX` | Entry645/642 synthetic Stage | lost ACK, no own new version, third-party interleave, post-ACK non-convergence, rollback readback all fail closed without unrelated rollback | `NOT_RUN` |
| `SUPPORTED_CLOUDFLARE_API_VERSION_TARGET` | Cloudflare version/settings contract | prove API support for explicit version lineage or enforce validated lock; never replace latest blindly | `NOT_RUN` |
| `GLOBAL_SINGLE_WRITER_CAS` | all Worker deploy writers | owner-enforced exclusive writer including external CLI/GitHub actions, or CAS equivalent that rejects stale writer before mutation | `NOT_RUN` |
| `REPO_WORKER_WRITER_INVENTORY` | all GitHub actions, scripts and manual deploys | complete target-worker writer inventory, owning agent and required lock, no source-only omission | `NOT_RUN` |
| `STAGE_CONCURRENT_DEPLOY` | disposable Worker | simulate two writers and prove safe rejection/lock; no active third-party overwrite | `NOT_RUN` |
| `STAGE_SETTINGS_DUPLICATES` | disposable Worker settings | duplicate input names and FILES URL collisions reject before API PATCH; source and actual Cloudflare staging behavior separated | `NOT_RUN` |
| `BUNDLE_SHA256_PROVENANCE` | build source→artifact→active Worker | pinned SHA, reproducible bundle, SHA256 hash, active Cloudflare code/ETag matched with same pipeline | `NOT_RUN` |
| `POSTDEPLOY_READONLY_WITNESS` | authorized production post-deploy | after owner-approved deployment only: signed redacted read-only witness, health/ACL/binding parity and rollback gate | `NOT_RUN` |
| `DISPOSABLE_STAGE` | entire proposed repair set on disposable Worker and fake data | all functional and security suites pass with no customer/finance records accessed | `NOT_RUN` |
| `CLOUD_LIVE_VERIFICATION` | live Worker active source/route | read-only evidence only with owner's permissions; distinguish from CI and Stage | `NOT_RUN` |
| `ACCOUNTING_OWNER_ATTESTATION` | EasyStore separate owner book | independent owner signs customer/accounting provenance and release; never assume finance enabled from TrendOS repair | `INDEPENDENT_OWNER_PENDING` |

**أولوية العمل عند الاستئناف:** أولًا اختبارات Node/الموجودة وYAML على نسخ PR SHA، ثم حصر العملاء المتأثرين بالواجهة/Mirror دون قراءة بيانات حساسة، ثم بيئة Stage صناعية مفصولة تثبت positive/negative للملفات والأذونات وربط R2/التراجع، ثم إثبات نسخة Worker وإعداداتها قراءةً فقط، وأخيرًا طلب موافقة المالك على أي نشر وتحقيق لاحق. لا تستخدم إنتاج العميل كبيئة اختبار.

```text
Status (لا يجوز CLOSED بدون إثبات):

تأكيد تحديث AGENT_LOG + Crosswalk + Manifest:
```

### ENTRY694-E — نقاط الاستكمال وسلطة المشروع

- **آخر مشروعات حارسة:** PR #221 (Zero Google) Draft `fca5625e53815647addc6d183ebe4040990acdf5`؛ PR #211 (EasyStore) Draft `682aa00181697ae241b6c9ede1ee854b80eca105` عند القراءة فقط؛ إصلاحات الأمن PR #234 وCloudflare PR #236 كلاهما Draft. أي تقدم جديد في PR211 يملكه إيجنت EasyStore ويُراجع من مصدره الأحدث؛ ممنوع تغيير الحسابات أو اعتبار Finance RELEASE=GO.
- **منع ضياع التعليمات الجديدة:** كل محادثة لاحقة تبدأ بقراءة أول صفحة من الكتاب النشط **و** البحث عن `ENTRY694` والإضافات الأحدث، ثم تعيد بناء قائمة `OPEN` من آخر حدث لكل ID. هذه القاعدة **إلزام توثيقي في Git** وليست ميزة آلية أو حماية GitHub Ruleset؛ لا يمكن ضمان التزام محادثة لا تقرأ الكتاب. الكتاب هو وسيلة الإرجاع المتفق عليها.
- **بواباتنا الصريحة الآن:** `PR234=FIX_IN_DRAFT`، `PR236=FIX_IN_DRAFT`، `ISOLATED_MOCKS=29/29`، `NATIVE_NODE_NEW=NOT_RUN`، `CI=NOT_RUN`، `STAGE=NOT_RUN`، `PRODUCTION_FIX_DEPLOYED=NO`، `CLOUDFLARE_DEPLOYED_SHA=UNVERIFIED`، `R2_ACTIVE_BINDING=UNVERIFIED`، `FINAL_CUTOVER=NO_GO`، `FINANCE_AUTHORITY=EASYSTORE`.
- **ممنوع فقد مرجع:** السجل القديم Entry645/692/693 يحتفظ بالتفاصيل الأصلية والأدلة الدقيقة؛ هذا الفصل مؤشر تشغيل دائم **وليس بديلاً** عن سجلات الحوادث أو كتاب واتس إيجنت أو كتب المطبعة المستقلة.


### ENTRY694-LEDGER-EVENT-20261024-01 — أول تشغيل فعلي لاختبارات الإصلاح بـGitHub Node + إصلاح اختبار Mirror القديم + تكامل PR234/PR236
- **سبب الاستكمال:** قاعدة المالك `ENTRY694` تقول إن فحص `SOURCE_ONLY` أو محاكاة داخل الشات لا يثبت `NATIVE_NODE` أو `CI`. راجعنا الكتاب النشط وHEAD `b586877c84984d89fc79964d7881349e4020fd24`، ولاحظنا أن نجاح Workflow `TrendOS A61 browser Cloud transport regression` على رأسَي PR #234 و#236 **لا يشمل** `entry692` أو `entry693`: سجلات A61 تذكر فحوص Frontend/Transport، لا الاختبارات الجديدة. لم نرقِّ مستوى الإثبات اعتمادًا على اسمه أو علامة النجاح فقط.
- **فحص CI فعلي جديد:** أضفنا نفس `.github/workflows/trendos-entry694-draft-security-regression.yml` إلى الفرعين المستقلين (read-only repo permissions، no deploy، checkout no persisted credentials، Node 22، بدون Secrets/Cloudflare/D1/R2 حقيقية). الـWorkflow ينفذ الملفات الجديدة `tests/entry692_private_read_guards.test.mjs` و/أو `tests/entry693_cloudflare_guard_source.test.mjs` الموجودة على الفرع، ثم مجموعة صغيرة محددة من اختبارات Entry614 Comms/Content وT12 read overlay وMirror safety.
- **مشكلة اختبار مكتشفة وسُجل فشلها قبل إصلاحها (SEC-692-03 / TEST-694-01):** أول CI على PR234 `06158fb0d1890708ff60889edf52ec869df62945` run **[40028920391](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/40028920391)** job `120671990470` = **FAIL**، لأن اختبار `tests/cloudflare_mirror_safety_v1.test.mjs:130` توقع HTTP 200 لطلب Mirror مجهول؛ الإصلاح الصحيح رد `401`. **اختبار `entry692` نفسه نجح خلال نفس التشغيل**. لم نضعف حماية Mirror؛ بدلًا من ذلك عدّلنا fixture بحيث يزود `x-mirror-read-secret` من متغير اختبار وهمي، وأضفنا أربع حالات رفض (بدون مفتاح، مفتاح خطأ، مفتاح الكتابة فقط، ومتغير القراءة غير معرف) مع إثبات عدم الوصول إلى D1 قبل الرفض. ### ACC245-OUTBOX-RECHECK-20261025 — revalidate CURRENT original T12 source before each signed outbox lease (SOURCE-ONLY / NO_GO)

**Owner responsibility / no overlap:** Continue existing TrendOS ↔ EasyStore integration safety branch PR #211 after ACC235-OUTBOX / Entry691. Separate finance accounting agent retains the independent EasyStore database, real money/invoice ledger and authorized Stage. This change did NOT touch EasyStore repo, its DB, T12/create writer, employee/accounting handlers, account credit, migration, Cloudflare secrets, transport, customer data, Worker routes, deploy, merge or Google systems.

**Closed isolated gap:** The original ACC235-OUTBOX SQLite fixture persisted immutable HMAC-signed `order_created` message and could re-lease old bytes after a crash. However, original T12 department status could be changed AFTER persistence, including cancellation, stopped/duplicate or mutation of source actor/customer/line/outbox. A valid HMAC verified the historical message, NOT the current T12 validity; retry had no fresh source check. No financial approval may be inferred from it.

**Code:** Reused ORIGINAL `inspectT12EmployeeOrderFinanceCandidate()` in the EXISTING fixture-only `cloudflare-d1/src/t12-finance-signed-outbox-acc235-offline.mjs` without new SQL schema, route, endpoint, finance writer or sender. `reserveSignedFixtureForSimulatedRetry` now **requires original `sourceDb`**, reads one fresh five-SELECT D1 batch after verifying stored payload hash and pinned HMAC, checks original create/ledger/activity/outbox/current native runtime through ACC228–234, and compares exact order ID, official customer **candidate** ID, original create timestamp and ordered complete source Print/Laser line IDs, ordinals and departments against the **persisted original signed bytes**. It **denies** missing/broken source DB, corrupt/reassigned/cancelled source, changed customer/line/created_at, or mismatch BEFORE conditional SQLite lease CAS/attempt increment. Healthy source permits the old isolated lease but retains `sourceOnly:true,consumerAcknowledged:false,financeAuthorized:false,postingAllowed:false,connectorEventPublished:false`. Never turns status/WhatsApp, static signed message or accounting line preview into independent verified ownership or signed financial `order_line_approved`.

**Evidence and regression:** Reused existing original T12 source/SQLite test file; adjusted six ACC235 fixture lease scenarios to pass the already-existing source fixture and added four genuinely new ACC245 negatives/positives: cancellation **after** signed enqueue must block lease and leave bytes/attempts unchanged; customer/line/time mutation after enqueue must block without consuming attempt and healthy restoration can regain ordinary lease; missing/rejected source batch or removed original outbox stops; successful fresh batch remains read-only and produces exact same immutable HMAC bytes. Code [`0a827542`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/0a82754241a67c9366d80e7aaa376cb745577fe4), initial test [`c386f6dd`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/c386f6ddea32fd73ce32fbbe19d1f33e27c531cb), test-only correction of an inaccurate cumulative lease-attempt expectation [`52a20c9b`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/52a20c9bcaf3cb81079d5f9db850ddcfc4612492). Final original standalone [ACC235 signed outbox CI run #40194352907](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/40194352907) SUCCESS with **61/61 passing**; **all 19/19 PR workflows successful on the exact tested final code HEAD** `52a20c9bcaf3cb81079d5f9db850ddcfc4612492`. No fake financial ack, real billing, production or public deployment.

**Limits explicitly OPEN:** Fresh source D1 read and SQLite fixture CAS are **separate transactions/databases**: cancellation can occur between the check and an eventual real dispatch (**TOCTOU**). A production-grade connector needs durable source revision/fencing and recipient-side version/cancellation checks, authorized issuer/key custody, official customer order owner grant, human-signed department approvals, independent EasyStore-only pricing/invoice and consumer replay/ACK journal. This is NOT a live delivery service or proof of production exactly-once semantics. `ACC245_SOURCE_RECHECK=OFFLINE_PASS`, `FINANCE_RELEASE=NO_GO`, `G1..G5=OPEN`. ### ACC246-OUTBOX-FENCE-20261025 — original T12 runtime revision + lease-epoch pre-dispatch gate (SOURCE-ONLY / NO_GO)

**Assignment and separation:** Existing original TrendOS ↔ EasyStore integration Draft PR211 continues after ACC245. Finance agent exclusively owns EasyStore database/Stage, official ledger/invoices and any payment authority. No EasyStore schema/DB, accounting native writer, original T12 CREATE/runtime writer, client dataset, Worker endpoint, Cloudflare secret, production deploy, migration, token, finance money write, Google or PR merge touched. Do not overwrite concurrent work or supersede any earlier ENTRY694 policy gate.

**Problem and precise scope:** ACC245 reread current T12 in a five-SELECT read-only batch **before claiming** a signed operational `order_created` fixture message, but an actor can legitimately change T12 runtime `t12_prod_line_runtime` **after lease**. A worker with a stale lease could otherwise continue without noticing changes. Cross-DB source read + detached SQLite fixture CAS are not one atomic source/receiver transaction; a post-gate race persists.

**Code:** Extend existing `cloudflare-d1/src/t12-employee-order-finance-intake-sourceonly.mjs` to return `operationalRevision` with native customer version and each line's native runtime `version` from the already-validated **same original 5-SELECT** snapshot (0 for original unmodified line). This is source-owned *untrusted-to-finance* read-only version evidence, NOT signer attestation, an approved employee, customer ownership, monetary data or part of the immutable HMAC V1 `order_created` payload. Native runtime writer monotonically advances `version` per status/WhatsApp event; original inspector continues to reject runtime count/status/actor drift.

**Fixture outbox fence:** Extend existing standalone temporary `ACC235_ISOLATED_SCHEMA` only, NOT production migrations: add mutable `lease_source_revision_json` and `simulated_handoff_epoch` while preserving the immutable signed JSON + SHA-256 + event/key identity triggers. Every CAS lease now stores a short exact ordered revision snapshot of current T12 line IDs/versions and customer version alongside owner, expiry, attempt. New **OFF-by-default** `simulateACC246PreDispatchFenceFixtureOnly` verifies pinned original message HMAC/TTL/hash again, validates current lease owner, attempt epoch and expiry, performs a second fresh source-only five-query original T12 read, compares complete original order/customer/line/time binding and the exact recorded source revision, and finally uses a conditional **SQLite `UPDATE ... RETURNING`** to mark at most ONE fixture-only handoff per lease attempt. If line cancelled, changed-then-returned to old status (native version increments: ABA), customer/line/source changes, missing reader, expired signature or stale/reclaimed lease: fail closed BEFORE simulated handoff marker. No external sender/HTTP `fetch`, authenticated receiver, acknowledgement, payment or invoice action. The new marker is not a real dispatch or consumer ACK.

**Observed CI evidence:** Reader patch [`204aa278`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/204aa278d3aaafa31a789f727eec5cf5caeb1185), isolated Outbox CAS module [`1de40afd`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/1de40afdf318470d397f3ee000c9ec35f13ef9aa), original disposable SQLite tests [`ceaec5c3`](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/commit/ceaec5c31d7c884b3eab8e39f7a7809d542d001f): 5 new tests for healthy immutable source/one handoff; cancellation *after* lease; native version counter ABA; expired/old owner lease fencing; fail-closed default OFF, missing source and HMAC expiry. Dedicated [signed source Outbox GitHub Actions run #40194989852](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/40194989852) **66/66 tests PASS**, and **all 19/19 current PR GitHub Actions SUCCESS on exact code HEAD `ceaec5c31d7c884b3eab8e39f7a7809d542d001f`**, no failure/pending. Existing ACC228–245 tests retained and passing, no new production endpoints.

**Unclosed/never claimed:** Native runtime revision protects against ordinary native line transition ABA **only**; tampering/revert of unversioned source columns, compromised source, clock delay or a change after second read can still occur. True live dispatch safety needs a producer-side native atomic revision fence or consumer-side authenticated version/revocation check with deterministic event binding, independently attested order/customer ownership, independently authorized authentic Wael/Gaber source-issued signed `order_line_approved`, independently provisioned EasyStore stage, durable committed source outbox, receiver dedupe/ACK/grants/prices and explicit owner GO. `ACC246_PRE_DISPATCH_FENCE=DISPOSABLE_SQLITE_VERIFIED`, `LIVE_SERVER_TO_SERVER_TRANSPORT=OFF`, `FINANCE_RELEASE=NO_GO`, `G1..G5=OPEN`.

### ACC254-PLATFORM-ORDER-SOURCE-REUSE-ARCH-20261025

**User architecture request:** Do NOT construct a competing SOURCE ORDER database for EasyStore; link to the already-running TrendOS platform's original order identities to prevent duplication or cross-origin customer/order confusion. This is an **architecture verification and proposed source-contract decision only**, not authorization for a live production read, schema change, Stage cutover, source customer import, or financial posting.

**Grounded actual platform schema/flow:** `cloudflare-d1/wrangler.toml` binds current platform Worker `trendos-d1-api` to operational **`trendos-main`** (UUID `5c4b92bf-e043-4f6e-bd6d-d514a92cd825`). Platform frontend `trendos-edge-orders-read-v1.js` routes new manual CREATE to Cloudflare T12 `/v1/t12/orders/create` and notes Cloud-native IDs from 4322 onward; actual native creator writes `t12_prod_request_ledger`, `t12_prod_orders`, `t12_prod_lines`, activity and operational outbox using original T12 create batch, with `request_key` idempotency. `t12-read-overlay.mjs` reads and joins `t12_prod_lines` ↔ `t12_prod_orders` and current runtime, exports `dataSource:'t12-prod-native'`, `writeAuthority:'cloudflare-t12'`. The existing page deliberately merges those Cloud-native rows with qualified **legacy Sheets mirror** `sheet_rows` from `0002_full_sheet_mirror.sql`; display duplicates are deduped by line identity but **a merged UI read is NOT a financial authority**. Independent older `0001_init.sql` `orders` is an import/upsert projection keyed `order_id`, not proof of the original committed T12 order+customer ownership or official departmental approval. A phone/name/Google mirror key cannot be treated as immutable official customer ID.

**Architectural decision proposed (NOT applied):** For **Cloud-native** orders the sole order truth is the already-existing authoritative `trendos-main.t12_prod_orders` and one-to-many `t12_prod_lines`, with original request-ledger `request_key`, current source revisions, customer at-create **candidate** provenance and later independent official owner/approval gates. Avoid copying/recreating this as a second competing source `t12_prod_orders` database solely for EasyStore. A future explicitly approved **PRIVATE READ-ONLY source Worker/service binding** should query the authoritative operational platform on the TrendOS side and return only vetted `source_system='trendos_t12_native'`, `order_id`, `line_id`, immutable `request_key`/source order created-at and native source version; finance recipient stores stable reference and immutable receipt rather than re-owning or editing source operational tables. Order source reference must include a trusted namespace **(source system, order ID)**; lines scoped to that exact order; authenticated customer↔order grant must be independent from phone/name. Exact idempotency must be keyed by trusted signed event ID and order/line; inconsistent, ambiguous, legacy-vs-native duplicate identity MUST STOP/QUARANTINE, not silently join across origins. Legacy/Google/Screens mirror rows remain an explicitly separate `legacy_mirror_unverified` path and MUST NOT produce invoice authority until individually resolved with official approved source-of-truth. Keep `order_created` effect `prepare_context_only`, not monetary approval.

### OWNER-20261025 — NATIVE CUSTOMER IN-FLIGHT READ EPOCH SECURITY FENCE, UI DESIGN DECOUPLED (STAGE OFF)
- **Source and ownership:** Owner explicitly requested continue Zero Google backend while starting a **SEPARATE new chat to modify the existing customer UI**, so no `index.html`, `app.js`, CSS or interface layout/behavior source is touched in this hardening branch. New source [Draft #252](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/pull/252), stacked exactly on last green source [Draft #250](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/pull/250) SHA `f5048c9bb657402112a1d68de7a80c2cd828fd02`. User may edit UI on an independent branch; no production master relocation, real Cloudflare D1 access, employees/T12/EasyStore/accounting changes, merging or deployment.
- **Security issue/fix:** Original Native adapter `customer-native-browser-transport-v0.js` records a token epoch, increments it on native login/logout and clears token immediately on logout; before this patch, authorized `/v1/customer/native/orders` and public content GET responses issued while a session was active could still resolve **after** the user logs out or a different customer logs in. Added `rejectStaleNativeRead(bearer,epoch)` to check captured epoch and bearer identity immediately after each JSON read and before returning sanitized customer order rows or any of the five read-only content views. Stale data raises `CUSTOMER_NATIVE_STALE_READ_REJECTED`; no fallback to legacy Google and no customer data returned. Existing auth/route and employee dispatcher unchanged; test seam supported only at initial epoch zero.
- **Genuine Chromium delayed-response test:** Extension to `tests/customer_stage_chromium_full_original_portal_local.cjs` against real disposable local Wrangler Worker/D1/SQLite DO, using the ORIGINAL customer UI: intercept and hold two successful authorized GET responses (own D1 orders + public D1 white-label/brand) while customer is logged in; run genuine UI logout with server-side D1 CAS revocation; release both responses **after logout** and assert the adapter refuses both. No real customer or production resources.
- **CI failure ledger:** Initial [CI #40204780361](https://github.com/fawakhry/TrendOs/actions/runs/40204780361) FAILED on test instrumentation only: helper release block was unintentionally inserted after the FIRST `entryView` readiness check before login, causing `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'splice')`.
